Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The tissue operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 962. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 457 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the tissue space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 962 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 457. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)
